- **verify**: Post-execution quality gate combining goal-backward verification and 8-track cleanup into one coherent skill. Adds GSD agents gsd-verifier (goal-backward), gsd-doc-verifier (doc accuracy), gsd-ui-auditor (UI review), and gsd-security-auditor (security review). - **verify-before-answer**: 在回答前核验可能变化或需要精确引用的信息,不凭训练记忆作答。当问题涉及当前事实、价格、估值、市值、营收、用户数、政策法规、人物职位、公司动态、产品功能、API 用法、版本号、技术规格、统计数据、市场份额或行业基准时,先通过 web_search、web_fetch、官方文档查询、conversation_search 等手段交叉核验,再作答。即使用户没有明确要求查询,只要回答会包含具体数字、人名职位、产品规格、政策条款或时间敏感信息,也要使用本 skill。包括投资数据合理性检查、官方一手来源优先和单一来源置信度声明。Use whenever Claude would otherwise rely on training memory for any factual claim—prices, current roles, product specs, regulations, statistics, company financials, API details, version numbers, or anything with a "current state." ([GarageDev1/alex-skill](https://github.com/GarageDev1/alex-skill/tree/HEAD/verify-before-answer))
- **verify-before-answering**: Check the referenced source and extract what it actually says before making any factual claim about it. Use this whenever a question concerns a specific document, dataset, file, report, spreadsheet, email thread, or other artifact; whenever source material is available or referenced and the answer will make factual claims; and whenever summarizing, interpreting, comparing, or answering questions about provided material. - **verify-before-asserting**: Verify before claiming, fixing, or shipping: reproduce first, watch the test fail, then fix. Trigger on 'the bug is', 'just fixed', 'false positive', module changes. - **verify-before-asserting**: Activates before stating a fact about a system, claiming a fix works, citing documented behaviour, or asking the owner a question the repo or live system could answer. ([joshualytle/Gnomonkit](https://github.com/joshualytle/Gnomonkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/verify-before-asserting) · ★1)
- **verify-before-asserting**: Check reality before making a factual claim about code, state, or infrastructure. Use before saying a file exists, a branch is clean, a route is live, a value was saved, or something "is not implemented" — and before reporting that a change works. Prevents confident false statements built from memory or pattern-matching. ([wwvuillemot/dev-ai-tools](https://github.com/wwvuillemot/dev-ai-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/practices/skills/verify-before-asserting) · ★1)
- **verify-before-asserting**: Use before stating, relying on, or hardcoding an external fact where being wrong carries real cost — legal, financial, medical, safety, regulatory, security, or production-reliability. Prevents hallucinated figures and fabricated citations. Triggers on asserting a specific figure, threshold, rate, date, dosage, limit, config default, citation, version, or rule from memory. ([AidenHiew/verify-before-asserting](https://github.com/AidenHiew/verify-before-asserting/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **verify-before-asserting**: Use when writing a concrete reference (env, path, command, version) into a plan or code: confirm it exists or mark it ASSUMPTION; never infer it. - **verify-before-building**: Check that the trunk doesn't already have a feature before you branch for it. - **verify-before-cite**: Live-verify work state before reporting or relying on it, and reconcile every place that state is recorded. Use before a status report, standup, handoff, or "what's open" answer; when a tracker, queue, memory note, or prior session says something is pending, merged, shipped, or blocked; after any merge, release, or promotion; or when records in two places disagree. - **verify-before-claim**: Operationalizes the "verify before stating as fact" rule for completeness/correctness claims about code — phrases like "this is all the files", "every case is covered", "nothing else uses X", "all accounted for", "this is safe/correct".
